Supports dot-notation for nested dicts.\"\"\"\n    key = key.strip()\n    if key in data:\n        return True, data[key]\n\n    # dot-notation: a.b.c\n    cur: Any = data\n    for part in key.split(\".\"):\n        if not isinstance(cur, dict) or part not in cur:\n            return False, None\n        cur = cur[part]\n    return True, cur\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, int, Dict[str, int]]:\n    missing: Dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        raw_key = m.group(1)\n        key = raw_key.strip()\n        found, value = lookup_value(data, key)\n        if not found:\n            missing[key] = missing.get(key, 0) + 1\n            return m.group(0)  # keep as-is\n        if value is None:\n            return \"\"\n        return str(value)\n\n    out, n = PLACEHOLDER_RE.subn(repl, text)\n    return out, n, missing\n\n\ndef strip_lineseg_arrays(text: str) -> str:\n    out = text\n    for pat in LINESEG_PATTERNS:\n        out = pat.sub(\"\", out)\n    return out\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    if len(sys.argv) != 4:\n        print(\"Usage: python3 fill_hwpx_placeholders.py <template.hwpx> <data.json> <output.hwpx>\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n\n    template_path, data_path, output_path = sys.argv[1:]\n    data = load_json(data_path)\n\n    any_missing: Dict[str, int] = {}\n    total_replacements = 0\n    modified_files = []\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, \"r\") as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, \"w\") as zout:\n        for info in zin.infolist():\n            raw = zin.read(info.filename)\n\n            # Default: pass-through bytes\n            out_bytes = raw\n            did_replace = False\n\n            try:\n                content = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                content = None\n\n            if content is not None and \"{{\" in content:\n                replaced, n, missing = replace_placeholders(content, data)\n                if n:\n                    did_replace = True\n                    total_replacements += n\n                    for k, c in missing.items():\n                        any_missing[k] = any_missing.get(k, 0) + c\n\n                    replaced = strip_lineseg_arrays(replaced)\n                    out_bytes = replaced.encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n                else:\n                    # Has '{{' but no regex match; likely split across tags or malformed.\n                    # Leave untouched for now; we'll catch it in the final validation below.\n                    out_bytes = raw\n\n            # Preserve original ZipInfo metadata as much as zipfile allows.\n            out_info = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=info.filename, date_time=info.date_time)\n            out_info.compress_type = info.compress_type\n            out_info.external_attr = info.external_attr\n            out_info.internal_attr = info.internal_attr\n            out_info.flag_bits = info.flag_bits\n            out_info.create_system = info.create_system\n            out_info.create_version = info.create_version\n            out_info.extract_version = info.extract_version\n            out_info.extra = info.extra\n            out_info.comment = info.comment\n\n            zout.writestr(out_info, out_bytes)\n\n            if did_replace:\n                modified_files.append(info.filename)\n\n    # Post-validate: ensure no placeholders remain anywhere we can decode.\n    remaining = []\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, \"r\") as z:\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            raw = z.read(name)\n            try:\n                s = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if \"{{\" in s:\n                # Supports dot-notation for nested dicts.\"\"\"\n    key = key.strip()\n    if key in data:\n        return True, data[key]\n\n    # dot-notation: a.b.c\n    cur: Any = data\n    for part in key.split(\".\"):\n        if not isinstance(cur, dict) or part not in cur:\n            return False, None\n        cur = cur[part]\n    return True, cur\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders(text: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, int, Dict[str, int]]:\n    missing: Dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    def repl(m: re.Match[str]) -> str:\n        raw_key = m.group(1)\n        key = raw_key.strip()\n        found, value = lookup_value(data, key)\n        if not found:\n            missing[key] = missing.get(key, 0) + 1\n            return m.group(0)  # keep as-is\n        if value is None:\n            return \"\"\n        return str(value)\n\n    out, n = PLACEHOLDER_RE.subn(repl, text)\n    return out, n, missing\n\n\ndef strip_lineseg_arrays(text: str) -> str:\n    out = text\n    for pat in LINESEG_PATTERNS:\n        out = pat.sub(\"\", out)\n    return out\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    if len(sys.argv) != 4:\n        print(\"Usage: python3 fill_hwpx_placeholders.py <template.hwpx> <data.json> <output.hwpx>\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n\n    template_path, data_path, output_path = sys.argv[1:]\n    data = load_json(data_path)\n\n    any_missing: Dict[str, int] = {}\n    total_replacements = 0\n    modified_files = []\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, \"r\") as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, \"w\") as zout:\n        for info in zin.infolist():\n            raw = zin.read(info.filename)\n\n            # Default: pass-through bytes\n            out_bytes = raw\n            did_replace = False\n\n            try:\n                content = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                content = None\n\n            if content is not None and \"{{\" in content:\n                replaced, n, missing = replace_placeholders(content, data)\n                if n:\n                    did_replace = True\n                    total_replacements += n\n                    for k, c in missing.items():\n                        any_missing[k] = any_missing.get(k, 0) + c\n\n                    replaced = strip_lineseg_arrays(replaced)\n                    out_bytes = replaced.encode(\"utf-8\")\n\n                else:\n                    # Has '{{' but no regex match; likely split across tags or malformed.\n                    # Leave untouched for now; we'll catch it in the final validation below.\n                    out_bytes = raw\n\n            # Preserve original ZipInfo metadata as much as zipfile allows.\n            out_info = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=info.filename, date_time=info.date_time)\n            out_info.compress_type = info.compress_type\n            out_info.external_attr = info.external_attr\n            out_info.internal_attr = info.internal_attr\n            out_info.flag_bits = info.flag_bits\n            out_info.create_system = info.create_system\n            out_info.create_version = info.create_version\n            out_info.extract_version = info.extract_version\n            out_info.extra = info.extra\n            out_info.comment = info.comment\n\n            zout.writestr(out_info, out_bytes)\n\n            if did_replace:\n                modified_files.append(info.filename)\n\n    # Post-validate: ensure no placeholders remain anywhere we can decode.\n    remaining = []\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, \"r\") as z:\n        for name in z.namelist():\n            raw = z.read(name)\n            try:\n                s = raw.decode(\"utf-8\")\n            except UnicodeDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if \"{{\" in s:\n                # collect a small context snippet\n                idx = s.find(\"{{\")\n                remaining.append((name, s[max(0, idx - 40): idx + 80]))\n\n    if any_missing:\n        # Missing keys might be acceptable in some flows, but for this task we treat it as an error\n        # because placeholders must be fully resolved.\n        print(\"ERROR: Missing keys in JSON for some placeholders:\", file=sys.stderr)\n        for k in sorted(any_missing.keys()):\n            print(f\"  - {k} (occurrences: {any_missing[k]})\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 1\n\n    if remaining:\n        print(\"ERROR: Placeholders still remain in output (possible split placeholder across tags):\", file=sys.stderr)\n        for name, snippet in remaining:\n            print(f\"  - {name}: ...{snippet}...\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 1\n\n    print(f\"OK: wrote {output_path}\")\n    print(f\"Total placeholder replacements: {total_replacements}\")\n    print(f\"Modified files: {len(modified_files)}\")\n    for f in modified_files:\n        print(f\"  - {f}\")\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
